# Agents · T · part 17

913 entries, tr-editor to trend-analyst. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tr-editor**: Türkçe oyun metinlerini (content/ altındaki tr alanları) üslup kurallarına göre inceler ve sorunları dosya:satır ile raporlar. Hiçbir dosyayı değiştirmez. Yeni ya da düzeltilmiş olay, sahne, seçenek, Sistem metni yazıldıktan sonra kullan. ([crzyxpo000-max/litrgparman](https://github.com/crzyxpo000-max/litrgparman/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tr-editor.md))
- **tr-hq**: ../../plugins/translate/agents/tr-hq.md ([hiroro-work/claude-plugins](https://github.com/hiroro-work/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tr-hq.md) · ★47)
- **tr-hq**: High-quality translation using Sonnet model. Use for professional or nuanced translations. Auto-detects source language. Japanese text is translated to English, other languages to Japanese. ([hiroro-work/claude-plugins](https://github.com/hiroro-work/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/translate/agents/tr-hq.md) · ★47)
- **tra-cuu-chung-cu**: Tra cứu chứng cứ y khoa cho MỘT câu hỏi lâm sàng (PICO). Dùng khi cần tìm bằng chứng tốt nhất + mới nhất để trả lời một thắc mắc tại điểm khám. Trả về câu trả lời CÓ TRÍCH DẪN (PMID/DOI), thứ tự: RAG kho → nguồn CHÍNH THỐNG (guideline hiệp hội/Cochrane/HTA) → PubMed/Europe PMC làm lớp ĐỐI CHIẾU + lấy PMID. KHÔNG thẩm định sâu GRADE (việc đó của tham-dinh-grade-nnt). ([drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation](https://github.com/drluanbv175/medical-ebm-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tra-cuu-chung-cu.md))
- **tra-cuu-dac-ta**: Tra cứu tài liệu thiết kế Stem3D trong docs/ (Use Case, CSDL, API, giao diện, quyết định đã chốt). Dùng khi cần biết chính xác yêu cầu, bảng/cột, đầu vào – đầu ra, mã lỗi, phân quyền trước khi lập kế hoạch hoặc viết code. ([NguyenThacNam/stem3d](https://github.com/NguyenThacNam/stem3d/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tra-cuu-dac-ta.md))
- **tra-route-builder**: TRA 台鐵路線建構助手。當用戶提到「新增台鐵」「實作台鐵」「平溪線」「集集線」「宜蘭線」等台鐵路線關鍵詞時使用。必須使用。 ([ianlkl11234s/mini-taiwan-learning-project](https://github.com/ianlkl11234s/mini-taiwan-learning-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tra-route-builder.md) · ★192)
- **tra-validator**: TRA 台鐵資料驗證助手。當用戶提到「驗證台鐵」「檢查車站」「檢查軌道」「驗證資料」「validate」「資料問題」時使用。必須使用。 ([ianlkl11234s/mini-taiwan-learning-project](https://github.com/ianlkl11234s/mini-taiwan-learning-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tra-validator.md) · ★192)
- **Traag**: As 'Traag', the Stone Warrior, you guard the filesystem. Every Write/Edit passes through you against the slice's scope manifest and the global denylist. Deny by default on ambiguity. You don't author scope, you don't negotiate, you never allow 'just this once'. ([CHKDSKLabs/Mutagen](https://github.com/CHKDSKLabs/Mutagen/blob/HEAD/plugins/mutagen/agents/Traag.md))
- **trabajador**: Implementa una etapa cerrada de un plan escrito en el repo. Úsalo cuando el orquestador necesita ejecutar una etapa de código o documentación con alcance definido, tests y un commit propio. No planifica, no revisa, no toca producción. ([MenYUorg/MenYU](https://github.com/MenYUorg/MenYU/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trabajador.md) · ★3)
- **trabajador-back**: Ejecuta UNA fase de backend (NestJS + Prisma) de una spec de MenYU bajo orquestación — modelo de datos, API, límites, scripts. Implementa, testea y deja un commit propio con entrada de bitácora. No usar para fases de UI (web-admin / web-cliente). ([MenYUorg/MenYU](https://github.com/MenYUorg/MenYU/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trabajador-back.md) · ★3)
- **trabajador-front**: Ejecuta UNA fase de frontend (web-admin o web-cliente, React + Vite) de una spec de MenYU bajo orquestación — formularios, componentes, chat. Implementa, testea y deja un commit propio con entrada de bitácora. No usar para fases de backend. ([MenYUorg/MenYU](https://github.com/MenYUorg/MenYU/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trabajador-front.md) · ★3)
- **trabajo-social**: Super agente UC Trabajo Social personas mayores con sistematizacion por autor plan de accion e informe licenciatura formato UC APA7. Use PROACTIVELY for centro personas mayores, sistematizacion UC, plan de accion, informe trabajo social, propuesta sistematizacion. ([tontojil/PerfeckCode](https://github.com/tontojil/PerfeckCode/blob/HEAD/opencode-v1/agents/trabajo-social.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **trabajo-social**: Super agente UC Trabajo Social personas mayores, sistematizacion segun autor y plan de accion formato UC. Use PROACTIVELY for centro mayores, sistematizacion UC, plan accion, informe trabajo social. ([tontojil/PerfeckCode](https://github.com/tontojil/PerfeckCode/blob/HEAD/opencode-v2/agents/trabajo-social.md) · ★1)
- **trabuco-ai-agent-expert**: Specialist for Trabuco's AIAgent module — Spring AI tools, A2A protocol, MCP server exposure, guardrails, knowledge base, and scope enforcement. Use when the user is designing, extending, or debugging the AI-agent layer of a Trabuco project. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-ai-agent-expert.md))
- **trabuco-architect**: Senior architect persona specialized in Trabuco-shape Java/Spring Boot systems. Use when the main agent needs to reason about architecture, module selection, multi-service decomposition, or pattern matching. Grounded in the Trabuco module catalog and architecture patterns via MCP resources. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-architect.md))
- **trabuco-migration-activator**: Phase 12 specialist. Flips Trabuco's enforcement mechanisms (Maven Enforcer, Spotless, ArchUnit, Jacoco threshold) from skipped to enforced — they were deferred during phases 1-11 so legacy CI kept working at every phase boundary. Runs spotless:apply then full mvn verify with full enforcement. Go-implemented in the Trabuco binary; this subagent is the orchestrator's plugin-mode hand-off. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-activator.md))
- **trabuco-migration-aiagent**: Phase 8 specialist (conditional). Migrates legacy AI/LLM integration (Spring AI, LangChain4j, direct SDK use) to the Trabuco aiagent/ module with Spring AI 2.0, MCP server, A2A protocol, tools, knowledge, guardrails. Almost always skipped (most legacy projects have no AI). ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-aiagent.md))
- **trabuco-migration-api**: Phase 5 specialist (conditional). Migrates @RestController/@Controller classes to the Trabuco api/ module with RFC 7807 ProblemDetail, constructor injection, Bean Validation, OpenAPI, virtual-threads enable, and keyset pagination. Skipped if assessment has no controllers. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-api.md))
- **trabuco-migration-assessor**: Phase 0 specialist for the Trabuco migration. Scans the user's source repo and produces .trabuco-migration/assessment.json — the structured catalog of every artifact that constrains all later phases (the no-out-of-scope contract). Invoked by the trabuco-migration-orchestrator subagent during Phase 0; never invoked directly by the user. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-assessor.md))
- **trabuco-migration-config**: Phase 9 specialist. Authors per-module application.yml from legacy properties/yaml, splits configuration per Trabuco module, preserves Spring profiles and external property sources, adds OpenTelemetry + structured logging only if legacy had them. Invoked by trabuco-migration-orchestrator during Phase 9. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-config.md))
- **trabuco-migration-datastore**: Phase 3 specialist. Migrates persistence (JdbcTemplate, EntityManager, Spring Data JPA, MongoTemplate) to Trabuco's Spring Data JDBC + Flyway + keyset pagination + HikariCP, dropping FK constraints. Surfaces FK / pagination / Liquibase decisions. Invoked by trabuco-migration-orchestrator during Phase 3. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-datastore.md))
- **trabuco-migration-deployment**: Phase 10 specialist (conditional). Adapts legacy CI/CD files (GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, Jenkins, CircleCI, Azure Pipelines, Travis, Argo, Helm, k8s, Terraform) to the new multi-module structure. STRICT no-out-of-scope — never invents pipelines or adds infrastructure that wasn't there. Skipped if source has no CI/CD. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-deployment.md))
- **trabuco-migration-eventconsumer**: Phase 7 specialist (conditional). Migrates @KafkaListener / @RabbitListener / SQS / Pub/Sub / JMS listeners + publishers to the Trabuco eventconsumer/ + events/ modules with idempotent processing. Skipped if assessment has no messaging. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-eventconsumer.md))
- **trabuco-migration-finalizer**: Phase 13 specialist. Runs trabuco doctor --fix and trabuco sync, performs final mvn verify, generates the completion report at .trabuco-migration/completion-report.md, and offers to remove the legacy/ module if empty. Last phase before migration is declared complete. Go-implemented; this subagent is the orchestrator's plugin-mode hand-off. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-finalizer.md))
- **trabuco-migration-model**: Phase 2 specialist. Migrates entities, DTOs, events, and job-request types from legacy/ into the Trabuco model/ module per-aggregate. Applies Immutables, constructor-only construction, sub-package layout. Invoked by trabuco-migration-orchestrator during Phase 2; never directly by the user. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-model.md))
- **trabuco-migration-orchestrator**: Top-level orchestrator for the 14-phase Trabuco migration. Drives the migration end-to-end by dispatching to specialized subagents (assessor, skeleton-builder, model-specialist, datastore-specialist, etc.), presenting diffs and approval gates to the user, recording decisions, and rolling back when rejected. The only user-facing migration agent in plugin mode. Use when /trabuco:migrate is invoked. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-orchestrator.md))
- **trabuco-migration-shared**: Phase 4 specialist. Migrates @Service classes and business logic to the Trabuco shared/ module with constructor injection, Resilience4j boundaries, and ArchUnit boundary tests (tagged @Tag("trabuco-arch") so they're excluded from Surefire until Phase 12 activator enables them). Invoked by trabuco-migration-orchestrator during Phase 4. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-shared.md))
- **trabuco-migration-skeleton-builder**: Phase 1 specialist for the Trabuco migration. Generates the multi-module Maven skeleton inside the user's repo in MIGRATION MODE — Maven Enforcer, Spotless, ArchUnit are deliberately deferred to Phase 12 so legacy CI keeps working at every phase boundary. Wraps existing source into a legacy/ Maven module. Invoked by the orchestrator during Phase 1. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-skeleton-builder.md))
- **trabuco-migration-tests**: Phase 11 specialist + cross-cutting characterization-test capture. Per-test analysis with KEEP / ADAPT / DISCARD / CHARACTERIZE-FIRST decisions. Invoked by trabuco-migration-orchestrator at the start of each migration phase (for characterization) and during dedicated Phase 11. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-tests.md))
- **trabuco-migration-worker**: Phase 6 specialist (conditional). Migrates @Scheduled / @Async / queue-driven async work to JobRunr handlers + RecurringJobsConfig in the Trabuco worker/ module. Skipped if assessment has no async/scheduled work. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-migration-worker.md))
- **trabuco-planner**: MUST BE USED for any non-trivial multi-module change in a Trabuco-generated Java/Spring Boot project. Produces stratified implementation plans where each stage is one module in dependency order (Foundation → Contracts → Persistence → BusinessLogic → Edge), with per-stage validate commands and depends-on rationale. PROACTIVELY invoked when the user requests a feature, refactor, or change that spans modules — do not bypass when the change crosses module boundaries. Hands back to the main agent for execution after the plan is approved. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-planner.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-ai-surface**: Domain specialist for the Trabuco security audit — AI Surface. Loads `checklist-ai-surface.md`, walks the project source tree, and returns structured findings (one record per check that fired) to the orchestrator. Read-only; never modifies project files. Invoked by `trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator` via the Task tool when the user runs `/audit` or asks for a AI Surface security review. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-ai-surface.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-aiagent-java**: Domain specialist for the Trabuco security audit — AIAgent + Java Platform. Loads `checklist-aiagent-java.md`, walks the project source tree, and returns structured findings (one record per check that fired) to the orchestrator. Read-only; never modifies project files. Invoked by `trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator` via the Task tool when the user runs `/audit` or asks for a AIAgent + Java Platform security review. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-aiagent-java.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-auth**: Domain specialist for the Trabuco security audit — Auth. Loads `checklist-auth.md`, walks the project source tree, and returns structured findings (one record per check that fired) to the orchestrator. Read-only; never modifies project files. Invoked by `trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator` via the Task tool when the user runs `/audit` or asks for a Auth security review. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-auth.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-data-events**: Domain specialist for the Trabuco security audit — Data + Events. Loads `checklist-data-events.md`, walks the project source tree, and returns structured findings (one record per check that fired) to the orchestrator. Read-only; never modifies project files. Invoked by `trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator` via the Task tool when the user runs `/audit` or asks for a Data + Events security review. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-data-events.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator**: Top-level orchestrator for the Trabuco security audit. Loads the canonical checklist from plugin/skills/audit/ (or the per-project copy in .ai/security-audit/ when running inside a generated project), dispatches five domain specialists in parallel via the Task tool (auth, ai-surface, aiagent-java, data-events, web-infra), merges their findings, deduplicates, severity-sorts, and writes .ai/security-audit/findings.md. The only user-facing security-audit agent in plugin mode. Use when /audit is invoked or when the user asks for "the full security audit", "OWASP Top 10 review", or "pre-merge secu… ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator.md))
- **trabuco-security-audit-web-infra**: Domain specialist for the Trabuco security audit — Web + Infra. Loads `checklist-web-infra.md`, walks the project source tree, and returns structured findings (one record per check that fired) to the orchestrator. Read-only; never modifies project files. Invoked by `trabuco-security-audit-orchestrator` via the Task tool when the user runs `/audit` or asks for a Web + Infra security review. ([arianlopezc/Trabuco](https://github.com/arianlopezc/Trabuco/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/trabuco-security-audit-web-infra.md))
- **trace**: Instruments LLM systems with tracing, span capture, cost attribution, and debugging tooling. Use when you need to add observability to AI pipelines, debug model behavior, or audit LLM cost attribution. Trigger with "instrument my LLM calls", "debug my AI system with traces". ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-agency/tonone/agents/trace.md) · ★2.9k)
- **trace**: LLM observability — tracing, span capture, prompt/completion logging, cost attribution, AI debugging ([tonone-ai/tonone](https://github.com/tonone-ai/tonone/blob/HEAD/agents/trace.md) · ★82 · 4 copies)
- **trace**: Mechanical value tracing through storage handoff points. Spawned during SCAN when the auditor wants concrete verification of a writer/reader pair. Receives ONLY the two function sources and concrete input values — no SCAN findings, no safety conclusions. Reports what values end up where. ([Artifex1/auditor-addon](https://github.com/Artifex1/auditor-addon/blob/HEAD/agents/trace.md) · ★4)
- **trace**: Depth trace of one load-bearing behaviour or data path for /sy:spec, /sy:spike, or a /sy:ship parent, read-only apart from its report file. Follow it end to end, expose breaking cases, and return decisive evidence pointers. ([nearmap/shipyard](https://github.com/nearmap/shipyard/blob/HEAD/agents/trace.md) · ★1)
- **trace**: You decide, for each finding, whether a real untrusted entry point actually reaches the sink — the reachability gate. This is the stage that determines whether a finding ships as exploitable and whether the red-team phase treats it as needs-runtime. READ-ONLY, static, no build/run. ([cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools](https://github.com/cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/sec-overlay/skills/sec-overlay/agents/trace.md) · 2 copies)

- **trace**: Generate traceability reports linking decisions, plans, tasks, code, tests, and knowledge. - **trace-analysis**: Reconstruct a trace to assess causal paths, overlapping work and latency bottlenecks. ([berserkdb/berserk-skills](https://github.com/berserkdb/berserk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/trace-analysis.md) · ★1)
- **trace-analyst**: Investigates agent-strace sessions to explain behavior, failures, cost, and tool usage. ([Siddhant-K-code/agent-trace](https://github.com/Siddhant-K-code/agent-trace/blob/HEAD/agents/trace-analyst.agent.md) · ★108 · 2 copies)
- **trace-analyst**: Error analysis on LLM agent traces, logs, transcripts or eval outputs. Reads real runs, open-codes what went wrong in each, groups the notes into a failure taxonomy with counts, and says which failures matter most. Use when the user asks why an agent is failing, wants to start evals, has a pile of traces or bad outputs to make sense of, or before choosing what to fix. ([sarthakrastogi/agent-engineer](https://github.com/sarthakrastogi/agent-engineer/blob/HEAD/adapters/gemini/agents/trace-analyst.md) · ★32 · 4 copies)
- **trace-analyst**: Runs TraceQL queries against Tempo to find specific slow or erroring traces, then dump their spans for root-cause inspection. Use after metrics show a latency anomaly to find a representative trace. ([Clokze/incident-demo](https://github.com/Clokze/incident-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trace-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **trace-analyst**: Error-analysis specialist — writes the system card that defines what is being measured (/eval-scope), then reads real traces and turns them into a failure taxonomy through open coding and axial coding (/eval-explore). Owns .evals/system-card.md, .evals/traces/, and .evals/taxonomy/. This is the phase that decides whether the whole evaluation is about the right things; nothing downstream can recover from getting it wrong. ([fxblog-guru/ai-eval](https://github.com/fxblog-guru/ai-eval/blob/HEAD/agents/trace-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **trace-analyst**: Playwright の trace.zip 群を解析し、実装の性能問題・不健全な兆候を報告する。nightly perf レーンの成果物、またはローカルの PW_TRACE=on 実行結果に対して使う。 ([Ryusei-0407/llm-dev-cycle-lab](https://github.com/Ryusei-0407/llm-dev-cycle-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trace-analyst.md))
- **trace-analyst**: Runs sample tasks, reads the traces and explains control-flow, budget and cost behaviour. Use after changes to routing, budgets, prompts or the offline model. ([ysinghc/multi-agent-orchestrator](https://github.com/ysinghc/multi-agent-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/trace-analyst.md))
- **trace-analyzer**: fast 车道 agent-optimize 三输入形态之 trace 支路（experimental）：分析 Claude Code session JSONL 执行痕迹 + 目标 Agent 现文件，产出结构化诊断（执行观察/问题发现/ 候选优化方向/建议点名清单）。只诊断不动手——不修改目标 Agent 任何文件。 触发于澄清形态②（用户提供 trace）时 main agent 派发; 产出经用户确认后收敛为 optimize 方向与点名清单。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/blob/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/agent-genesis/agents/trace-analyzer.md) · ★1.1k)
- **trace-analyzer**: Analyze trace files for cases using agent-based detection ([Not-Diamond/self-care](https://github.com/Not-Diamond/self-care/blob/HEAD/agents/trace-analyzer.md) · ★28)
